{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — struct_self_consist_ie\\n\\n> Updated 2026-05-14 post-exp-016 pivot (D019). CRITICAL NARRATIVE CHANGE: best-of-N selection fails on NER (all signals ≤ greedy). Pivot from \\\"TTS improvement\\\" to \\\"confidence estimation + correlation-selection gap\\\".\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (via ARR May 2026)\\n- Fallback: EMNLP 2026 Findings\\n- Deadline: ARR May cycle, 2026-05-25\\n\\n## Core Claims (3 条)\\n\\n### Claim 1 (Positive — Quality Estimation)\\nStructural consistency signals (soft Jaccard, entity voting confidence) are the strongest unsupervised quality estimators for entity-level IE, significantly outperforming model confidence (log-probability) and surface agreement (FK, exact-match) at instance-level quality prediction. Structural consistency family achieves AUROC≈0.70 vs logprob 0.59 for NER discrimination. SJ and voting confidence are statistically comparable on NER (ρ≈0.39), SJ is temperature-robust (Δρ=±0.01).\\n\\n**Key insight**: The contribution is about a FAMILY of structural consistency signals for QUALITY ESTIMATION (predicting which instances are reliable), not for per-sample selection. SJ and voting_conf both capture entity-across-sample consistency. SJ generalizes to arbitrary structured outputs; voting is simpler.\\n\\n**Evidence** (SciERC, N=8, T=1.0):\\n- NER full (n=529): voting_conf ρ=0.392/τ=0.291/AUROC=0.704, SJ ρ=0.383/τ=0.266/AUROC=0.689, EM ρ=0.294/AUROC=0.640, FK ρ=0.259/AUROC=0.629, logprob ρ=0.140/AUROC=0.591\\n- NER cond: SJ ρ=0.412 (unique signal >0.40)\\n- Structural consistency family (SJ+voting) AUROC ~0.70 vs logprob 0.59 — both ρ and discriminative power confirm dominance\\n- RE full: voting_conf ρ=0.340/AUROC=0.667 > SJ ρ=0.244/AUROC=0.592 (voting more robust with sparse tuples)\\n- Temperature robustness: SJ Δρ=±0.01 across T=0.7/1.0; FK Δρ=±0.06-0.09 (SJ far more stable)\\n\\n### Claim 2 (Novel Finding — Correlation-Selection Gap)\\nInstance-level quality estimation ≠ per-sample selection ability. Despite strong instance-level correlation (ρ≈0.39-0.43), NO unsupervised confidence signal beats greedy decoding for NER best-of-N selection. On RE, only log-probability (+2.4pp over greedy) enables effective selection. The oracle headroom is large (NER +16pp, RE +18pp) but current signals cannot exploit it.\\n\\n**exp-016 evidence** (SciERC, N=8, T=1.0, n=529):\\n- NER best-of-N: greedy 0.6094 > logprob 0.6087 > ensemble 0.6043 > sj 0.6042 > voting 0.5959 > random 0.5796 (oracle 0.7692)\\n- RE best-of-N: logprob 0.3971 > greedy 0.3735 > sj 0.3680 > voting 0.3379 ≈ random 0.3380 (oracle 0.5610)\\n- Instance-level AUROC: voting_conf 0.700, SJ 0.689, ensemble 0.735 >> logprob 0.591\\n\\n**Key insight**: Consistency signals are INSTANCE-LEVEL aggregates (one score per instance). They predict which inputs are easy/hard, NOT which sample is best. Log-prob is the only PER-SAMPLE signal and uniquely enables selection on uncertain tasks (RE). This distinction between quality estimation and sample selection is the paper's most practically important finding.\\n\\n**Implications**: (a) For selective prediction / abstention → use structural consistency (AUROC 0.70+). (b) For best-of-N selection → use logprob, but only on tasks where greedy is suboptimal (RE). (c) For NER, greedy is near-optimal → TTS offers no advantage with current signals.\\n\\n### Claim 3 (Diagnostic — task-complexity boundary)\\nThe effectiveness of consistency-based confidence is governed by task structural complexity: compound error propagation in relational tasks degrades the consistency-accuracy correlation on the full instance set, but the signal remains valid within the model's capability range. This boundary applies across all confidence signals tested.\\n\\n**MVP evidence**: RE full rho_sj=0.234, RE cond rho_sj=0.372 (SJ>FK +11.8pp). Component decomposition shows all RE components equally weak (head_sj rho=0.210). T=0.7 naturally separates knowledge-gap vs uncertainty errors.\\n\\n**exp-005 evidence**: Conditional filtering dwarfs temperature tuning — RE SJ cond lift +0.07~0.14 vs temperature effect ±0.01. T=1.0 preserves more oracle headroom (RE: 0.167 vs 0.131) and diversity (1-SJ: 0.389 vs 0.313). Recommendation: T=1.0 + conditional filtering, not temperature optimization.\\n\\n**exp-001 N-scaling evidence** (SciERC, n=200 subset, ⚠️ needs full-set confirmation):\\n- RE N=16: voting_conf ρ=0.448 (vs N=8 0.340, +0.108), SJ ρ=0.333 (vs 0.244, +0.089), all signals improve +0.07~0.10\\n- NER N=16: flat (already saturated at N=8) — voting_conf 0.401 vs 0.392\\n- **Key insight**: The task-complexity boundary is partially a SAMPLE BUDGET boundary. Compound error propagation increases per-instance variance, requiring more samples (N=16 vs N=8) to average out noise. This makes Claim 2 more nuanced: RE consistency fails at N=8 not because the signal is absent, but because it's noisier and needs more samples to manifest.\\n- ⚠️ Caveat: exp-001 ran on 200/529 instances. Full-set replication needed before claiming in paper.\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n**Problem**: LLM-based structured prediction (IE) increasingly uses multi-sample generation, but practitioners lack guidance on which confidence signals to trust — and for what purpose. Crucially, it is unknown whether signals that predict output quality (instance-level) also enable effective sample selection (per-sample-level).\\n\\n**Existing approaches & gaps**: Self-consistency in reasoning uses exact-match voting (Wang et al. 2023). USC (Chen et al. 2024) uses LLM-as-judge. For structured prediction, no systematic comparison exists across confidence signal families. More importantly, prior work conflates quality estimation with selection — assuming that high correlation with accuracy translates to effective best-of-N selection.\\n\\n**Our framing**: We systematically compare five confidence signals across four families for IE: (1) model confidence (log-probability), (2) surface agreement (FK, exact-match), (3) set-aware structural similarity (SJ), (4) entity-level majority voting. We make three findings:\\n1. Structural consistency (SJ/voting_conf) provides the strongest quality estimation (AUROC 0.70 vs logprob 0.59)\\n2. However, a **correlation-selection gap** exists: despite strong instance-level correlation, NO signal beats greedy decoding for NER selection; only logprob helps for RE (+2.4pp)\\n3. All signals degrade on relational tasks due to compound error propagation\\n\\n**Headline result template**: \\\"On NER, structural consistency achieves AUROC 0.70 for quality estimation, significantly outperforming log-probability (0.59). However, this quality estimation capability does NOT translate to effective best-of-N selection — no signal surpasses greedy decoding on NER (oracle headroom: +16pp). We identify a fundamental correlation-selection gap: instance-level consistency predicts WHICH INPUTS are reliable, not WHICH OUTPUTS are best. On RE, only per-sample log-probability enables selection (+2.4pp), while all consistency signals degrade.\\\"\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n### R1: \\\"Only one NER dataset — generalization unclear\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-002 (CoNLL-2003) + exp-003 (WNUT-17) validate cross-dataset across 3 domains (science/news/social media)\\n- **Priority**: P0\\n\\n### R2: \\\"Only one model family (Qwen3-8B)\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-007 (LLaMA-3.1-8B) shows model-agnostic effect\\n- **Priority**: P0\\n\\n### R3: \\\"FK is a strawman baseline — what about log-prob, USC, exact-match?\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-012 (log-prob), exp-013 (exact-match), exp-014 (entity voting) provide comprehensive baseline comparison. Results: structural consistency family (SJ+voting) >> log-prob >> FK ≈ exact-match. USC discussed in related work (LLM-as-judge, orthogonal approach).\\n- **Priority**: P0, RESOLVED — results decisive\\n\\n### R3b: \\\"Voting confidence matches or beats SJ — why use SJ?\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: SJ generalizes to arbitrary structured outputs (relations, events, any set-structured prediction). Voting is entity-specific. NER: statistically equivalent. RE: voting more robust with sparse tuples. Paper presents both as valid instantiations of the structural consistency principle.\\n- **Priority**: Addressed by framing\\n\\n### R4: \\\"No actual TTS improvement demonstrated\\\"\\n- **Status**: ⚠️ CONFIRMED NEGATIVE by exp-016. Best-of-N selection DOES NOT beat greedy on NER with any signal. RE: only logprob +2.4pp over greedy.\\n- **Mitigation**: Paper PIVOTS to \\\"confidence estimation + correlation-selection gap analysis.\\\" exp-006 redesigned as diagnostic: show selection curves to visualize the gap (F1 vs N per signal + oracle ceiling). The gap itself is a contribution, not a failure.\\n- **Priority**: P0 — narrative pivot required\\n\\n### R5: \\\"Sample size N=8 may be too small for reliable SJ estimation\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-001 (N=16) with multi-seed robustness\\n- **Priority**: P0\\n\\n### R6: \\\"Conditional analysis cherry-picks favorable instances\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: Principled separation (knowledge-gap vs uncertainty). Add threshold sensitivity analysis. Temperature analysis provides independent validation.\\n- **Priority**: P0 (add sensitivity analysis)\\n\\n### R7: \\\"Only ρ as evaluation metric\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: Add AUROC, calibration plots, Kendall's τ for all experiments\\n- **Priority**: P0\\n\\n### R8: \\\"Formula-implementation discrepancy\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: Paper must describe actual algorithm (type-grouped Hungarian matching with max(|g_a|,|g_b|) denominator)\\n- **Priority**: P0, paper writing fix\\n\\n### R9: \\\"Evaluation circularity (T>0 consistency vs T=0 F1)\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: Add leave-one-out SJ evaluation as robustness check. Justify cross-mode design (greedy = deterministic reference, standard in TTS literature).\\n- **Priority**: P0\\n\\n### R10: \\\"Insufficient related work\\\" (三档一致 [B][M][W])\\n- **Mitigation**: Expand to 35+ references. Must cite:\\n  - USC (Chen 2024) — empirical differentiation, not just citation\\n  - Semantic uncertainty (Kuhn 2023), calibration (Kadavath 2022, Guo 2017, Desai & Durrett 2020)\\n  - **Conformal prediction for structured outputs**: Fisch et al. 2021, Angelopoulos et al. 2021, Bates et al. 2021\\n  - **Token-level NER uncertainty**: Xiao & Wang 2019, Shelmanov et al. 2021, He et al. 2020\\n  - **Ensemble/MC-dropout**: Lakshminarayanan et al. 2017, Gal & Ghahramani 2016\\n  - **Verifier/reward models**: Cobbe et al. 2021, Lightman et al. 2023\\n  - **TTS scaling theory**: Snell et al. 2024, Brown et al. 2024\\n  - **Structured calibration**: Kuleshov & Liang 2015, Jagannatha & Yu 2022\\n  - **IE confidence**: Culotta & McCallum 2004\\n  - SelfCheckGPT (Manakul 2023), selective prediction (Geifman & El-Yaniv 2017)\\n  - **[2025-2026 新颖性自查补充 — 必引]**:\\n    - CISC (ACL Findings 2025) — confidence-weighted majority vote for reasoning; 对比：我们用 structural similarity 而非 confidence 加权，且面向 set-structured IE 输出\\n    - Self-Certainty Best-of-N (ICLR 2026, arxiv 2502.18581) — KL-divergence reward-free selection; 对比：我们发现 log-prob 是唯一有效 selection signal (与其方向互补)，且揭示 correlation-selection gap\\n    - ReASC (2026, arxiv 2601.02970) — reliability-aware adaptive self-consistency; 对比：仍限于 reasoning 离散答案\\n    - Atomic Self-Consistency (EMNLP 2024) — 子声明级一致性 for long-form text; 对比：我们在 entity-set 级别做结构化度量\\n    - **Nédellec & Xia (Genomics Informatics, 2026-02, PMC12888769)** — \\\"Consistency–accuracy correlation in hard-prompted LLMs for entity and relation extraction\\\"。最直接竞争者：4 LLMs 在植物健康 NER/RE 上研究 consistency-accuracy 相关性。**关键差异**：(a) 他们用 surface-level string matching，自承\\\"overlooks semantically consistent outputs\\\"，我们的 SJ 正好解决此问题; (b) 不研究 best-of-N selection; (c) 单领域 hard-prompted (T=0); (d) 无多信号家族对比。论文 Intro 可引用其 limitation 作为 SJ 的动机\\n  - **[2025-2026 新颖性自查补充 — 应引]**:\\n    - UQ for LLMs Survey (ACL Findings 2025) — 最全面 LLM 不确定性综述\\n    - UQ+Calibration Survey (arxiv 2503.15850, 2025) — 第二篇综合综述\\n    - Semantic Entropy Probes (2024) — 低成本不确定性估计\\n    - Latent Self-Consistency (2025, arxiv 2508.18395) — embedding 空间一致性\\n    - Ranked Voting SC (2025, arxiv 2505.10772) — 排名投票变体，仍限于推理任务\\n    - Know Your Limits: Abstention Survey (TACL 2025) — 选择性预测综述，与 Claim 1 selective prediction 角度直接相关\\n    - Confiden\\n\\n...       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — struct_self_consist_ie\\n\\n> Updated 2026-05-14 post-exp-016 pivot (D019). CRITICAL NARRATIVE CHANGE: best-of-N selection fails on NER (all signals ≤ greedy). Pivot from \\\"TTS improvement\\\" to \\\"confidence estimation + correlation-selection gap\\\".\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (via ARR May 2026)\\n- Fallback: EMNLP 2026 Findings\\n- Deadline: ARR May cycle, 2026-05-25\\n\\n## Core Claims (3 条)\\n\\n### Claim 1 (Positive — Quality Estimation)\\nStructural consistency signals (soft Jaccard, entity voting confidence) are the strongest unsupervised quality estimators for entity-level IE, significantly outperforming model confidence (log-probability) and surface agreement (FK, exact-match) at instance-level quality prediction. Structural consistency family achieves AUROC≈0.70 vs logprob 0.59 for NER discrimination. SJ and voting confidence are statistically comparable on NER (ρ≈0.39), SJ is temperature-robust (Δρ=±0.01).\\n\\n**Key insight**: The contribution is about a FAMILY of structural consistency signals for QUALITY ESTIMATION (predicting which instances are reliable), not for per-sample selection. SJ and voting_conf both capture entity-across-sample consistency. SJ generalizes to arbitrary structured outputs; voting is simpler.\\n\\n**Evidence** (SciERC, N=8, T=1.0):\\n- NER full (n=529): voting_conf ρ=0.392/τ=0.291/AUROC=0.704, SJ ρ=0.383/τ=0.266/AUROC=0.689, EM ρ=0.294/AUROC=0.640, FK ρ=0.259/AUROC=0.629, logprob ρ=0.140/AUROC=0.591\\n- NER cond: SJ ρ=0.412 (unique signal >0.40)\\n- Structural consistency family (SJ+voting) AUROC ~0.70 vs logprob 0.59 — both ρ and discriminative power confirm dominance\\n- RE full: voting_conf ρ=0.340/AUROC=0.667 > SJ ρ=0.244/AUROC=0.592 (voting more robust with sparse tuples)\\n- Temperature robustness: SJ Δρ=±0.01 across T=0.7/1.0; FK Δρ=±0.06-0.09 (SJ far more stable)\\n\\n### Claim 2 (Novel Finding — Correlation-Selection Gap)\\nInstance-level quality estimation ≠ per-sample selection ability. Despite strong instance-level correlation (ρ≈0.39-0.43), NO unsupervised confidence signal beats greedy decoding for NER best-of-N selection. On RE, only log-probability (+2.4pp over greedy) enables effective selection. The oracle headroom is large (NER +16pp, RE +18pp) but current signals cannot exploit it.\\n\\n**exp-016 evidence** (SciERC, N=8, T=1.0, n=529):\\n- NER best-of-N: greedy 0.6094 > logprob 0.6087 > ensemble 0.6043 > sj 0.6042 > voting 0.5959 > random 0.5796 (oracle 0.7692)\\n- RE best-of-N: logprob 0.3971 > greedy 0.3735 > sj 0.3680 > voting 0.3379 ≈ random 0.3380 (oracle 0.5610)\\n- Instance-level AUROC: voting_conf 0.700, SJ 0.689, ensemble 0.735 >> logprob 0.591\\n\\n**Key insight**: Consistency signals are INSTANCE-LEVEL aggregates (one score per instance). They predict which inputs are easy/hard, NOT which sample is best. Log-prob is the only PER-SAMPLE signal and uniquely enables selection on uncertain tasks (RE). This distinction between quality estimation and sample selection is the paper's most practically important finding.\\n\\n**Implications**: (a) For selective prediction / abstention → use structural consistency (AUROC 0.70+). (b) For best-of-N selection → use logprob, but only on tasks where greedy is suboptimal (RE). (c) For NER, greedy is near-optimal → TTS offers no advantage with current signals.\\n\\n### Claim 3 (Diagnostic — task-complexity boundary)\\nThe effectiveness of consistency-based confidence is governed by task structural complexity: compound error propagation in relational tasks degrades the consistency-accuracy correlation on the full instance set, but the signal remains valid within the model's capability range. This boundary applies across all confidence signals tested.\\n\\n**MVP evidence**: RE full rho_sj=0.234, RE cond rho_sj=0.372 (SJ>FK +11.8pp). Component decomposition shows all RE components equally weak (head_sj rho=0.210). T=0.7 naturally separates knowledge-gap vs uncertainty errors.\\n\\n**exp-005 evidence**: Conditional filtering dwarfs temperature tuning — RE SJ cond lift +0.07~0.14 vs temperature effect ±0.01. T=1.0 preserves more oracle headroom (RE: 0.167 vs 0.131) and diversity (1-SJ: 0.389 vs 0.313). Recommendation: T=1.0 + conditional filtering, not temperature optimization.\\n\\n**exp-001 N-scaling evidence** (SciERC, n=200 subset, ⚠️ needs full-set confirmation):\\n- RE N=16: voting_conf ρ=0.448 (vs N=8 0.340, +0.108), SJ ρ=0.333 (vs 0.244, +0.089), all signals improve +0.07~0.10\\n- NER N=16: flat (already saturated at N=8) — voting_conf 0.401 vs 0.392\\n- **Key insight**: The task-complexity boundary is partially a SAMPLE BUDGET boundary. Compound error propagation increases per-instance variance, requiring more samples (N=16 vs N=8) to average out noise. This makes Claim 2 more nuanced: RE consistency fails at N=8 not because the signal is absent, but because it's noisier and needs more samples to manifest.\\n- ⚠️ Caveat: exp-001 ran on 200/529 instances. Full-set replication needed before claiming in paper.\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n**Problem**: LLM-based structured prediction (IE) increasingly uses multi-sample generation, but practitioners lack guidance on which confidence signals to trust — and for what purpose. Crucially, it is unknown whether signals that predict output quality (instance-level) also enable effective sample selection (per-sample-level).\\n\\n**Existing approaches & gaps**: Self-consistency in reasoning uses exact-match voting (Wang et al. 2023). USC (Chen et al. 2024) uses LLM-as-judge. For structured prediction, no systematic comparison exists across confidence signal families. More importantly, prior work conflates quality estimation with selection — assuming that high correlation with accuracy translates to effective best-of-N selection.\\n\\n**Our framing**: We systematically compare five confidence signals across four families for IE: (1) model confidence (log-probability), (2) surface agreement (FK, exact-match), (3) set-aware structural similarity (SJ), (4) entity-level majority voting. We make three findings:\\n1. Structural consistency (SJ/voting_conf) provides the strongest quality estimation (AUROC 0.70 vs logprob 0.59)\\n2. However, a **correlation-selection gap** exists: despite strong instance-level correlation, NO signal beats greedy decoding for NER selection; only logprob helps for RE (+2.4pp)\\n3. All signals degrade on relational tasks due to compound error propagation\\n\\n**Headline result template**: \\\"On NER, structural consistency achieves AUROC 0.70 for quality estimation, significantly outperforming log-probability (0.59). However, this quality estimation capability does NOT translate to effective best-of-N selection — no signal surpasses greedy decoding on NER (oracle headroom: +16pp). We identify a fundamental correlation-selection gap: instance-level consistency predicts WHICH INPUTS are reliable, not WHICH OUTPUTS are best. On RE, only per-sample log-probability enables selection (+2.4pp), while all consistency signals degrade.\\\"\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n### R1: \\\"Only one NER dataset — generalization unclear\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-002 (CoNLL-2003) + exp-003 (WNUT-17) validate cross-dataset across 3 domains (science/news/social media)\\n- **Priority**: P0\\n\\n### R2: \\\"Only one model family (Qwen3-8B)\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-007 (LLaMA-3.1-8B) shows model-agnostic effect\\n- **Priority**: P0\\n\\n### R3: \\\"FK is a strawman baseline — what about log-prob, USC, exact-match?\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-012 (log-prob), exp-013 (exact-match), exp-014 (entity voting) provide comprehensive baseline comparison. Results: structural consistency family (SJ+voting) >> log-prob >> FK ≈ exact-match. USC discussed in related work (LLM-as-judge, orthogonal approach).\\n- **Priority**: P0, RESOLVED — results decisive\\n\\n### R3b: \\\"Voting confidence matches or beats SJ — why use SJ?\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: SJ generalizes to arbitrary structured outputs (relations, events, any set-structured prediction). Voting is entity-specific. NER: statistically equivalent. RE: voting more robust with sparse tuples. Paper presents both as valid instantiations of the structural consistency principle.\\n- **Priority**: Addressed by framing\\n\\n### R4: \\\"No actual TTS improvement demonstrated\\\"\\n- **Status**: ⚠️ CONFIRMED NEGATIVE by exp-016. Best-of-N selection DOES NOT beat greedy on NER with any signal. RE: only logprob +2.4pp over greedy.\\n- **Mitigation**: Paper PIVOTS to \\\"confidence estimation + correlation-selection gap analysis.\\\" exp-006 redesigned as diagnostic: show selection curves to visualize the gap (F1 vs N per signal + oracle ceiling). The gap itself is a contribution, not a failure.\\n- **Priority**: P0 — narrative pivot required\\n\\n### R5: \\\"Sample size N=8 may be too small for reliable SJ estimation\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-001 (N=16) with multi-seed robustness\\n- **Priority**: P0\\n\\n### R6: \\\"Conditional analysis cherry-picks favorable instances\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: Principled separation (knowledge-gap vs uncertainty). Add threshold sensitivity analysis. Temperature analysis provides independent validation.\\n- **Priority**: P0 (add sensitivity analysis)\\n\\n### R7: \\\"Only ρ as evaluation metric\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: Add AUROC, calibration plots, Kendall's τ for all experiments\\n- **Priority**: P0\\n\\n### R8: \\\"Formula-implementation discrepancy\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: Paper must describe actual algorithm (type-grouped Hungarian matching with max(|g_a|,|g_b|) denominator)\\n- **Priority**: P0, paper writing fix\\n\\n### R9: \\\"Evaluation circularity (T>0 consistency vs T=0 F1)\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: Add leave-one-out SJ evaluation as robustness check. Justify cross-mode design (greedy = deterministic reference, standard in TTS literature).\\n- **Priority**: P0\\n\\n### R10: \\\"Insufficient related work\\\" (三档一致 [B][M][W])\\n- **Mitigation**: Expand to 35+ references. Must cite:\\n  - USC (Chen 2024) — empirical differentiation, not just citation\\n  - Semantic uncertainty (Kuhn 2023), calibration (Kadavath 2022, Guo 2017, Desai & Durrett 2020)\\n  - **Conformal prediction for structured outputs**: Fisch et al. 2021, Angelopoulos et al. 2021, Bates et al. 2021\\n  - **Token-level NER uncertainty**: Xiao & Wang 2019, Shelmanov et al. 2021, He et al. 2020\\n  - **Ensemble/MC-dropout**: Lakshminarayanan et al. 2017, Gal & Ghahramani 2016\\n  - **Verifier/reward models**: Cobbe et al. 2021, Lightman et al. 2023\\n  - **TTS scaling theory**: Snell et al. 2024, Brown et al. 2024\\n  - **Structured calibration**: Kuleshov & Liang 2015, Jagannatha & Yu 2022\\n  - **IE confidence**: Culotta & McCallum 2004\\n  - SelfCheckGPT (Manakul 2023), selective prediction (Geifman & El-Yaniv 2017)\\n  - **[2025-2026 新颖性自查补充 — 必引]**:\\n    - CISC (ACL Findings 2025) — confidence-weighted majority vote for reasoning; {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — struct_self_consist_ie\\n\\n> Updated 2026-05-14 post-exp-016 pivot (D019). CRITICAL NARRATIVE CHANGE: best-of-N selection fails on NER (all signals ≤ greedy). Pivot from \\\"TTS improvement\\\" to \\\"confidence estimation + correlation-selection gap\\\".\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (via ARR May 2026)\\n- Fallback: EMNLP 2026 Findings\\n- Deadline: ARR May cycle, 2026-05-25\\n\\n## Core Claims (3 条)\\n\\n### Claim 1 (Positive — Quality Estimation)\\nStructural consistency signals (soft Jaccard, entity voting confidence) are the strongest unsupervised quality estimators for entity-level IE, significantly outperforming model confidence (log-probability) and surface agreement (FK, exact-match) at instance-level quality prediction. Structural consistency family achieves AUROC≈0.70 vs logprob 0.59 for NER discrimination. SJ and voting confidence are statistically comparable on NER (ρ≈0.39), SJ is temperature-robust (Δρ=±0.01).\\n\\n**Key insight**: The contribution is about a FAMILY of structural consistency signals for QUALITY ESTIMATION (predicting which instances are reliable), not for per-sample selection. SJ and voting_conf both capture entity-across-sample consistency. SJ generalizes to arbitrary structured outputs; voting is simpler.\\n\\n**Evidence** (SciERC, N=8, T=1.0):\\n- NER full (n=529): voting_conf ρ=0.392/τ=0.291/AUROC=0.704, SJ ρ=0.383/τ=0.266/AUROC=0.689, EM ρ=0.294/AUROC=0.640, FK ρ=0.259/AUROC=0.629, logprob ρ=0.140/AUROC=0.591\\n- NER cond: SJ ρ=0.412 (unique signal >0.40)\\n- Structural consistency family (SJ+voting) AUROC ~0.70 vs logprob 0.59 — both ρ and discriminative power confirm dominance\\n- RE full: voting_conf ρ=0.340/AUROC=0.667 > SJ ρ=0.244/AUROC=0.592 (voting more robust with sparse tuples)\\n- Temperature robustness: SJ Δρ=±0.01 across T=0.7/1.0; FK Δρ=±0.06-0.09 (SJ far more stable)\\n\\n### Claim 2 (Novel Finding — Correlation-Selection Gap)\\nInstance-level quality estimation ≠ per-sample selection ability. Despite strong instance-level correlation (ρ≈0.39-0.43), NO unsupervised confidence signal beats greedy decoding for NER best-of-N selection. On RE, only log-probability (+2.4pp over greedy) enables effective selection. The oracle headroom is large (NER +16pp, RE +18pp) but current signals cannot exploit it.\\n\\n**exp-016 evidence** (SciERC, N=8, T=1.0, n=529):\\n- NER best-of-N: greedy 0.6094 > logprob 0.6087 > ensemble 0.6043 > sj 0.6042 > voting 0.5959 > random 0.5796 (oracle 0.7692)\\n- RE best-of-N: logprob 0.3971 > greedy 0.3735 > sj 0.3680 > voting 0.3379 ≈ random 0.3380 (oracle 0.5610)\\n- Instance-level AUROC: voting_conf 0.700, SJ 0.689, ensemble 0.735 >> logprob 0.591\\n\\n**Key insight**: Consistency signals are INSTANCE-LEVEL aggregates (one score per instance). They predict which inputs are easy/hard, NOT which sample is best. Log-prob is the only PER-SAMPLE signal and uniquely enables selection on uncertain tasks (RE). This distinction between quality estimation and sample selection is the paper's most practically important finding.\\n\\n**Implications**: (a) For selective prediction / abstention → use structural consistency (AUROC 0.70+). (b) For best-of-N selection → use logprob, but only on tasks where greedy is suboptimal (RE). (c) For NER, greedy is near-optimal → TTS offers no advantage with current signals.\\n\\n### Claim 3 (Diagnostic — task-complexity boundary)\\nThe effectiveness of consistency-based confidence is governed by task structural complexity: compound error propagation in relational tasks degrades the consistency-accuracy correlation on the full instance set, but the signal remains valid within the model's capability range. This boundary applies across all confidence signals tested.\\n\\n**MVP evidence**: RE full rho_sj=0.234, RE cond rho_sj=0.372 (SJ>FK +11.8pp). Component decomposition shows all RE components equally weak (head_sj rho=0.210). T=0.7 naturally separates knowledge-gap vs uncertainty errors.\\n\\n**exp-005 evidence**: Conditional filtering dwarfs temperature tuning — RE SJ cond lift +0.07~0.14 vs temperature effect ±0.01. T=1.0 preserves more oracle headroom (RE: 0.167 vs 0.131) and diversity (1-SJ: 0.389 vs 0.313). Recommendation: T=1.0 + conditional filtering, not temperature optimization.\\n\\n**exp-001 N-scaling evidence** (SciERC, n=200 subset, ⚠️ needs full-set confirmation):\\n- RE N=16: voting_conf ρ=0.448 (vs N=8 0.340, +0.108), SJ ρ=0.333 (vs 0.244, +0.089), all signals improve +0.07~0.10\\n- NER N=16: flat (already saturated at N=8) — voting_conf 0.401 vs 0.392\\n- **Key insight**: The task-complexity boundary is partially a SAMPLE BUDGET boundary. Compound error propagation increases per-instance variance, requiring more samples (N=16 vs N=8) to average out noise. This makes Claim 2 more nuanced: RE consistency fails at N=8 not because the signal is absent, but because it's noisier and needs more samples to manifest.\\n- ⚠️ Caveat: exp-001 ran on 200/529 instances. Full-set replication needed before claiming in paper.\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n**Problem**: LLM-based structured prediction (IE) increasingly uses multi-sample generation, but practitioners lack guidance on which confidence signals to trust — and for what purpose. Crucially, it is unknown whether signals that predict output quality (instance-level) also enable effective sample selection (per-sample-level).\\n\\n**Existing approaches & gaps**: Self-consistency in reasoning uses exact-match voting (Wang et al. 2023). USC (Chen et al. 2024) uses LLM-as-judge. For structured prediction, no systematic comparison exists across confidence signal families. More importantly, prior work conflates quality estimation with selection — assuming that high correlation with accuracy translates to effective best-of-N selection.\\n\\n**Our framing**: We systematically compare five confidence signals across four families for IE: (1) model confidence (log-probability), (2) surface agreement (FK, exact-match), (3) set-aware structural similarity (SJ), (4) entity-level majority voting. We make three findings:\\n1. Structural consistency (SJ/voting_conf) provides the strongest quality estimation (AUROC 0.70 vs logprob 0.59)\\n2. However, a **correlation-selection gap** exists: despite strong instance-level correlation, NO signal beats greedy decoding for NER selection; only logprob helps for RE (+2.4pp)\\n3. All signals degrade on relational tasks due to compound error propagation\\n\\n**Headline result template**: \\\"On NER, structural consistency achieves AUROC 0.70 for quality estimation, significantly outperforming log-probability (0.59). However, this quality estimation capability does NOT translate to effective best-of-N selection — no signal surpasses greedy decoding on NER (oracle headroom: +16pp). We identify a fundamental correlation-selection gap: instance-level consistency predicts WHICH INPUTS are reliable, not WHICH OUTPUTS are best. On RE, only per-sample log-probability enables selection (+2.4pp), while all consistency signals degrade.\\\"\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n### R1: \\\"Only one NER dataset — generalization unclear\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-002 (CoNLL-2003) + exp-003 (WNUT-17) validate cross-dataset across 3 domains (science/news/social media)\\n- **Priority**: P0\\n\\n### R2: \\\"Only one model family (Qwen3-8B)\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-007 (LLaMA-3.1-8B) shows model-agnostic effect\\n- **Priority**: P0\\n\\n### R3: \\\"FK is a strawman baseline — what about log-prob, USC, exact-match?\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-012 (log-prob), exp-013 (exact-match), exp-014 (entity voting) provide comprehensive baseline comparison. Results: structural consistency family (SJ+voting) >> log-prob >> FK ≈ exact-match. USC discussed in related work (LLM-as-judge, orthogonal approach).\\n- **Priority**: P0, RESOLVED — results decisive\\n\\n### R3b: \\\"Voting confidence matches or beats SJ — why use SJ?\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: SJ generalizes to arbitrary structured outputs (relations, events, any set-structured prediction). Voting is entity-specific. NER: statistically equivalent. RE: voting more robust with sparse tuples. Paper presents both as valid instantiations of the structural consistency principle.\\n- **Priority**: Addressed by framing\\n\\n### R4: \\\"No actual TTS improvement demonstrated\\\"\\n- **Status**: ⚠️ CONFIRMED NEGATIVE by exp-016. Best-of-N selection DOES NOT beat greedy on NER with any signal. RE: only logprob +2.4pp over greedy.\\n- **Mitigation**: Paper PIVOTS to \\\"confidence estimation + correlation-selection gap analysis.\\\" exp-006 redesigned as diagnostic: show selection curves to visualize the gap (F1 vs N per signal + oracle ceiling). The gap itself is a contribution, not a failure.\\n- **Priority**: P0 — narrative pivot required\\n\\n### R5: \\\"Sample size N=8 may be too small for reliable SJ estimation\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-001 (N=16) with multi-seed robustness\\n- **Priority**: P0\\n\\n### R6: \\\"Conditional analysis cherry-picks favorable instances\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: Principled separation (knowledge-gap vs uncertainty). Add threshold sensitivity analysis. Temperature analysis provides independent validation.\\n- **Priority**: P0 (add sensitivity analysis)\\n\\n### R7: \\\"Only ρ as evaluation metric\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: Add AUROC, calibration plots, Kendall's τ for all experiments\\n- **Priority**: P0\\n\\n### R8: \\\"Formula-implementation discrepancy\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: Paper must describe actual algorithm (type-grouped Hungarian matching with max(|g_a|,|g_b|) denominator)\\n- **Priority**: P0, paper writing fix\\n\\n### R9: \\\"Evaluation circularity (T>0 consistency vs T=0 F1)\\\"\\n- **Mitigation**: Add leave-one-out SJ evaluation as robustness check. Justify cross-mode design (greedy = deterministic reference, standard in TTS literature).\\n- **Priority**: P0\\n\\n### R10: \\\"Insufficient related work\\\" (三档一致 [B][M][W])\\n- **Mitigation**: Expand to 35+ references. Must cite:\\n  - USC (Chen 2024) — empirical differentiation, not just citation\\n  - Semantic uncertainty (Kuhn 2023), calibration (Kadavath 2022, Guo 2017, Desai & Durrett 2020)\\n  - **Conformal prediction for structured outputs**: Fisch et al. 2021, Angelopoulos et al. 2021, Bates et al. 2021\\n  - **Token-level NER uncertainty**: Xiao & Wang 2019, Shelmanov et al. 2021, He et al. 2020\\n  - **Ensemble/MC-dropout**: Lakshminarayanan et al. 2017, Gal & Ghahramani 2016\\n  - **Verifier/reward models**: Cobbe et al. 2021, Lightman et al. 2023\\n  - **TTS scaling theory**: Snell et al. 2024, Brown et al. 2024\\n  - **Structured calibration**: Kuleshov & Liang 2015, Jagannatha & Yu 2022\\n  - **IE confidence**: Culotta & McCallum 2004\\n  - SelfCheckGPT (Manakul 2023), selective prediction (Geifman & El-Yaniv 2017)\\n  - **[2025-2026 新颖性自查补充 — 必引]**:\\n    - CISC (ACL Findings 2025) — confidence-weighted majority vote for reasoning; 对比：我们用 structural similarity 而非 confidence 加权，且面向 set-structured IE 输出\\n    - Self-Certainty Best-of-N (ICLR 2026, arxiv 2502.18581) — KL-divergence reward-free selection; 对比：我们发现 log-prob 是唯一有效 selection signal (与其方向互补)，且揭示 correlation-selection gap\\n    - ReASC (2026, arxiv 2601.02970) — reliability-aware adaptive self-consistency; 对比：仍限于 reasoning 离散答案\\n    - Atomic Self-Consistency (EMNLP 2024) — 子声明级一致性 for long-form text; 对比：我们在 entity-set 级别做结构化度量\\n    - **Nédellec & Xia (Genomics Informatics, 2026-02, PMC12888769)** — \\\"Consistency–accuracy correlation in hard-prompted LLMs for entity and relation extraction\\\"。最直接竞争者：4 LLMs 在植物健康 NER/RE 上研究 consistency-accuracy 相关性。**关键差异**：(a) 他们用 surface-level string matching，自承\\\"overlooks semantically consistent outputs\\\"，我们的 SJ 正好解决此问题; (b) 不研究 best-of-N selection; (c) 单领域 hard-prompted (T=0); (d) 无多信号家族对比。论文 Intro 可引用其 limitation 作为 SJ 的动机\\n  - **[2025-2026 新颖性自查补充 — 应引]**:\\n    - UQ for LLMs Survey (ACL Findings 2025) — 最全面 LLM 不确定性综述\\n    - UQ+Calibration Survey (arxiv 2503.15850, 2025) — 第二篇综合综述\\n    - Semantic Entropy Probes (2024) — 低成本不确定性估计\\n    - Latent Self-Consistency (2025, arxiv 2508.18395) — embedding 空间一致性\\n    - Ranked Voting SC (2025, arxiv 2505.10772) — 排名投票变体，仍限于推理任务\\n    - Know Your Limits: Abstention Survey (TACL 2025) — 选择性预测综述，与 Claim 1 selective prediction 角度直接相关\\n    - Confidence Estimation Survey (NAACL 2024, arxiv 2311.08298) — LLM 置信度估计全景定位\\n    - **CoCoA (Vashurin et al., NeurIPS 2025, arxiv 2502.04964)** — MBR 框架融合 confidence+consistency 做 UQ，在 QA/摘要/翻译上评估。与我们 exp-016 ensemble (SJ+logprob) 对标，但未涉及 structured IE\\n    - **Vashurin et al. (arxiv 2510.20460, 2025)** — VCE/MSP/Sample Consistency/CoCoA 四方法系统评估，任务不含 structured IE\\n  - **需新增 Related Work 小节**:\\n    - \\\"Test-Time Scaling & Sample Selection\\\" — 将 CISC/Self-Certainty/ReASC 集中讨论，明确 positioning：现有工作均面向 reasoning/QA 的离散答案，我们首次在 structured IE 上做系统信号对比并发现 correlation-selection gap\\n    - \\\"Consistency for Structured IE\\\" — 引 Nédellec & Xia 2026 作为同期工作，显式对比：surface matching vs structural metrics (SJ)；他们的 limitation 是我们的 motivation\\n- **Priority**: P0, Paper Agent handles\\n\\n### R11: \\\"SJ+logprob ensemble missing\\\" (Dream 2 — both reviewers)\\n- **Status**: ✅ RESOLVED by exp-016. Results:\\n  - Ensemble AUROC: NER 0.735 (+3.5pp over best single), RE 0.719 (−0.2pp, no improvement)\\n  - Linear combo: SJ+voting best at α=0.3 (NER ρ=0.489); RE α=0.0 (pure voting)\\n  - FK negative coefficient in logistic regression → surface agreement is net-negative when structural signals present\\n  - **Interpretation**: voting_conf captures nearly all discriminative information; ensemble adds marginal value on NER, none on RE. Supports the \\\"structural consistency family\\\" narrative.\\n- **Priority**: DONE — write into paper with honest interpretation\\n\\n### R12: \\\"No supervised upper bound / reward model comparison\\\" (Dream 2)\\n- **Mitigation**: Simple logistic regression on [SJ, FK, logprob, voting_conf, EM] as proxy for trained verifier (included in exp-016). Full reward model training out of scope for this paper — discuss as future work.\\n- **Priority**: P1 (partially addressed by exp-016 ensemble)\\n\\n### R13: \\\"Headroom capture rate low (37% NER, 11% RE)\\\" (Dream 2)\\n- **Mitigation**: Discuss explicitly in paper. Contextualize vs other unsupervised TTS methods. Argue that the information-theoretic ceiling for unsupervised signals is unknown — our result establishes a baseline.\\n- **Priority**: P0, paper writing\\n\\n### R14: \\\"Cross-model validation thin — need more than 2×8B\\\" (Dream 2)\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-017 (LLaMA on CoNLL, P1). 70B model infeasible within GPU budget. Acknowledge as limitation.\\n- **Priority**: P1\\n\\n### R15: \\\"Log-prob definition ambiguity — Eq.1 vs best-of-N ranking\\\" (Dream 2 cross-tier, [W])\\n- **Mitigation**: Paper must clarify: log-prob ranks among N candidates (each sample's generation log-prob), not just score greedy output. Cost comparison table must state \\\"all best-of-N require N forward passes; log-prob adds zero ranking overhead.\\\"\\n- **Priority**: P0, Paper Agent handles\\n\\n### R16: \\\"Zero figures — data-rich paper with only tables\\\" (Dream 2 cross-tier, [W])\\n- **Mitigation**: Paper Agent must include ≥3 figures: (1) selection curves (F1 vs N for each signal + oracle), (2) reliability diagram (calibration), (3) N-scaling or cost-effectiveness scatter. Data exists from exp-010/015.\\n- **Priority**: P0, Paper Agent handles\\n\\n### R17: \\\"Diagnostic framework overclaimed — descriptive not prescriptive\\\" (Dream 2 cross-tier, [M][W])\\n- **Mitigation**: Soften \\\"diagnostic framework\\\" to \\\"diagnostic analysis\\\" throughout. Current taxonomy (ceiling/floor/compound error) is descriptive. Either formalize with a priori metrics or reframe as empirical analysis.\\n- **Priority**: P0, Paper Agent handles\\n\\n### R18: \\\"No discussion of when consistency WOULD help\\\" (Dream 2 cross-tier, [W])\\n- **Mitigation**: Add discussion paragraph: consistency should outperform log-prob when (a) model calibration is poor (in-context learning without fine-tuning), (b) log-prob unavailable (closed-source APIs), (c) ensemble/mixture settings, (d) tasks where model is systematically overconfident.\\n- **Priority**: P1, Paper Agent handles\\n\\n### R19: \\\"Negative result partially predictable for fine-tuned models\\\" (Dream 2 cross-tier, [W])\\n- **Mitigation**: Paper must argue non-obviousness: (a) self-consistency demonstrably helps for reasoning tasks (Wang et al. 2023), (b) structured prediction has internal structure that surface metrics can't capture, (c) the DEGREE of log-prob's strength and the task-complexity boundary are not predictable a priori.\\n- **Priority**: P1, Paper Agent handles\\n\\n## Venue Fit\\n\\nEMNLP is well-suited for:\\n- Systematic empirical comparison of confidence signals for structured prediction\\n- Diagnostic findings with principled explanations\\n- The \\\"comparison + boundary condition\\\" contribution pattern\\n\\nRisk assessment (post-Dream 2, all tiers 3/6):\\n- **Structural risks (三档一致)**: (1) novelty perceived as incremental, (2) related work thin, (3) single RE dataset. These are presentation/positioning issues, NOT data issues — fixable by Paper Agent.\\n- **Data-dependent risks (观察中)**: SJ vs LOGPROB ranking may be dataset-dependent (Dream imagined this; awaiting exp-002/003 real data). If confirmed → reframe as \\\"complementary signals\\\" rather than \\\"SJ dominates.\\\"\\n- **Confirmed strength**: SJ >> LOGPROB on SciERC (AUROC 0.70 vs 0.59). Structural consistency family consistently top on existing data.\\n- **Critical path**: Paper Agent (related work 35+ refs, 3+ figures, contribution reframe) > new GPU experiments.\\n\", \"total_chars\": 17212, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

The oracle headroom is large (NER +16pp, RE +18pp) but current signals cannot exploit it.\\\\n\\\\n**exp-016 evidence** (SciERC, N=8, T=1.0, n=529):\\\\n- NER best-of-N: greedy 0.6094 > logprob 0.6087 > ensemble 0.6043 > sj 0.6042 > voting 0.5959 > random 0.5796 (oracle 0.7692)\\\\n- RE best-of-N: logprob 0.3971 > greedy 0.3735 > sj 0.3680 > voting 0.3379 ≈ random 0.3380 (oracle 0.5610)\\\\n- Instance-level AUROC: voting_conf 0.700, SJ 0.689, ensemble 0.735 >> logprob 0.591\\\\n\\\\n**Key insight**: Consistency signals are INSTANCE-LEVEL aggregates (one score per instance). They predict which inputs are easy/hard, NOT which sample is best. Log-prob is the only PER-SAMPLE signal and uniquely enables selection on uncertain tasks (RE). This distinction between quality estimation and sample selection is the paper's most practically important finding.\\\\n\\\\n**Implications**: (a) For selective prediction / abstention → use structural consistency (AUROC 0.70+). (b) For best-of-N selection → use logprob, but only on tasks where greedy is suboptimal (RE). (c) For NER, greedy is near-optimal → TTS offers no advantage with current signals.\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 3 (Diagnostic — task-complexity boundary)\\\\nThe effectiveness of consistency-based confidence is governed by task structural complexity: compound error propagation in relational tasks degrades the consistency-accuracy correlation on the full instance set, but the signal remains valid within the model's capability range. This boundary applies across all confidence signals tested.\\\\n\\\\n**MVP evidence**: RE full rho_sj=0.234, RE cond rho_sj=0.372 (SJ>FK +11.8pp). Component decomposition shows all RE components equally weak (head_sj rho=0.210). T=0.7 naturally separates knowledge-gap vs uncertainty errors.\\\\n\\\\n**exp-005 evidence**: Conditional filtering dwarfs temperature tuning — RE SJ cond lift +0.07~0.14 vs temperature effect ±0.01. T=1.0 preserves more oracle headroom (RE: 0.167 vs 0.131) and diversity (1-SJ: 0.389 vs 0.313). Recommendation: T=1.0 + conditional filtering, not temperature optimization.\\\\n\\\\n**exp-001 N-scaling evidence** (SciERC, n=200 subset, ⚠️ needs full-set confirmation):\\\\n- RE N=16: voting_conf ρ=0.448 (vs N=8 0.340, +0.108), SJ ρ=0.333 (vs 0.244, +0.089), all signals improve +0.07~0.10\\\\n- NER N=16: flat (already saturated at N=8) — voting_conf 0.401 vs 0.392\\\\n- **Key insight**: The task-complexity boundary is partially a SAMPLE BUDGET boundary. Compound error propagation increases per-instance variance, requiring more samples (N=16 vs N=8) to average out noise. This makes Claim 2 more nuanced: RE consistency fails at N=8 not because the signal is absent, but because it's noisier and needs more samples to manifest.\\\\n- ⚠️ Caveat: exp-001 ran on 200/529 instances. Full-set replication needed before claiming in paper.\\\\n\\\\n## Story Spine\\\\n\\\\n**Problem**: LLM-based structured prediction (IE) increasingly uses multi-sample generation, but practitioners lack guidance on which confidence signals to trust — and for what purpose. Crucially, it is unknown whether signals that predict output quality (instance-level) also enable effective sample selection (per-sample-level).\\\\n\\\\n**Existing approaches & gaps**: Self-consistency in reasoning uses exact-match voting (Wang et al. 2023). USC (Chen et al. 2024) uses LLM-as-judge. For structured prediction, no systematic comparison exists across confidence signal families. More importantly, prior work conflates quality estimation with selection — assuming that high correlation with accuracy translates to effective best-of-N selection.\\\\n\\\\n**Our framing**: We systematically compare five confidence signals across four families for IE: (1) model confidence (log-probability), (2) surface agreement (FK, exact-match), (3) set-aware structural similarity (SJ), (4) entity-level majority voting. We make three findings:\\\\n1. Structural consistency (SJ/voting_conf) provides the strongest quality estimation (AUROC 0.70 vs logprob 0.59)\\\\n2. However, a **correlation-selection gap** exists: despite strong instance-level correlation, NO signal beats greedy decoding for NER selection; only logprob helps for RE (+2.4pp)\\\\n3. All signals degrade on relational tasks due to compound error propagation\\\\n\\\\n**Headline result template**: \\\\\\\"On NER, structural consistency achieves AUROC 0.70 for quality estimation, significantly outperforming log-probability (0.59). However, this quality estimation capability does NOT translate to effective best-of-N selection — no signal surpasses greedy decoding on NER (oracle headroom: +16pp). We identify a fundamental correlation-selection gap: instance-level consistency predicts WHICH INPUTS are reliable, not WHICH OUTPUTS are best. On RE, only per-sample log-probability enables selection (+2.4pp), while all consistency signals degrade.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\\\n\\\\n### R1: \\\\\\\"Only one NER dataset — generalization unclear\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-002 (CoNLL-2003) + exp-003 (WNUT-17) validate cross-dataset across 3 domains (science/news/social media)\\\\n- **Priority**: P0\\\\n\\\\n### R2: \\\\\\\"Only one model family (Qwen3-8B)\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-007 (LLaMA-3.1-8B) shows model-agnostic effect\\\\n- **Priority**: P0\\\\n\\\\n### R3: \\\\\\\"FK is a strawman baseline — what about log-prob, USC, exact-match?\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-012 (log-prob), exp-013 (exact-match), exp-014 (entity voting) provide comprehensive baseline comparison. Results: structural consistency family (SJ+voting) >> log-prob >> FK ≈ exact-match. USC discussed in related work (LLM-as-judge, orthogonal approach).\\\\n- **Priority**: P0, RESOLVED — results decisive\\\\n\\\\n### R3b: \\\\\\\"Voting confidence matches or beats SJ — why use SJ?\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **Mitigation**: SJ generalizes to arbitrary structured outputs (relations, events, any set-structured prediction). Voting is entity-specific. NER: statistically equivalent. RE: voting more robust with sparse tuples. Paper presents both as valid instantiations of the structural consistency principle.\\\\n- **Priority**: Addressed by framing\\\\n\\\\n### R4: \\\\\\\"No actual TTS improvement demonstrated\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **Status**: ⚠️ CONFIRMED NEGATIVE by exp-016. Best-of-N selection DOES NOT beat greedy on NER with any signal. RE: only logprob +2.4pp over greedy.\\\\n- **Mitigation**: Paper PIVOTS to \\\\\\\"confidence estimation + correlation-selection gap analysis.\\\\\\\" exp-006 redesigned as diagnostic: show selection curves to visualize the gap (F1 vs N per signal + oracle ceiling). The gap itself is a contribution, not a failure.\\\\n- **Priority**: P0 — narrative pivot required\\\\n\\\\n### R5: \\\\\\\"Sample size N=8 may be too small for reliable SJ estimation\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-001 (N=16) with multi-seed robustness\\\\n- **Priority**: P0\\\\n\\\\n### R6: \\\\\\\"Conditional analysis cherry-picks favorable instances\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **Mitigation**: Principled separation (knowledge-gap vs uncertainty). Add threshold sensitivity analysis. Temperature analysis provides independent validation.\\\\n- **Priority**: P0 (add sensitivity analysis)\\\\n\\\\n### R7: \\\\\\\"Only ρ as evaluation metric\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **Mitigation**: Add AUROC, calibration plots, Kendall's τ for all experiments\\\\n- **Priority**: P0\\\\n\\\\n### R8: \\\\\\\"Formula-implementation discrepancy\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **Mitigation**: Paper must describe actual algorithm (type-grouped Hungarian matching with max(|g_a|,|g_b|) denominator)\\\\n- **Priority**: P0, paper writing fix\\\\n\\\\n### R9: \\\\\\\"Evaluation circularity (T>0 consistency vs T=0 F1)\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **Mitigation**: Add leave-one-out SJ evaluation as robustness check. Justify cross-mode design (greedy = deterministic reference, standard in TTS literature).\\\\n- **Priority**: P0\\\\n\\\\n### R10: \\\\\\\"Insufficient related work\\\\\\\" (三档一致 [B][M][W])\\\\n- **Mitigation**: Expand to 35+ references. Must cite:\\\\n  - USC (Chen 2024) — empirical differentiation, not just citation\\\\n  - Semantic uncertainty (Kuhn 2023), calibration (Kadavath 2022, Guo 2017, Desai & Durrett 2020)\\\\n  - **Conformal prediction for structured outputs**: Fisch et al. 2021, Angelopoulos et al. 2021, Bates et al. 2021\\\\n  - **Token-level NER uncertainty**: Xiao & Wang 2019, Shelmanov et al. 2021, He et al. 2020\\\\n  - **Ensemble/MC-dropout**: Lakshminarayanan et al. 2017, Gal & Ghahramani 2016\\\\n  - **Verifier/reward models**: Cobbe et al. 2021, Lightman et al. 2023\\\\n  - **TTS scaling theory**: Snell et al. 2024, Brown et al. 2024\\\\n  - **Structured calibration**: Kuleshov & Liang 2015, Jagannatha & Yu 2022\\\\n  - **IE confidence**: Culotta & McCallum 2004\\\\n  - SelfCheckGPT (Manakul 2023), selective prediction (Geifman & El-Yaniv 2017)\\\\n  - **[2025-2026 新颖性自查补充 — 必引]**:\\\\n    - CISC (ACL Findings 2025) — confidence-weighted majority vote for reasoning; 对比：我们用 structural similarity 而非 confidence 加权，且面向 set-structured IE 输出\\\\n    - Self-Certainty Best-of-N (ICLR 2026, arxiv 2502.18581) — KL-divergence reward-free selection; 对比：我们发现 log-prob 是唯一有效 selection signal (与其方向互补)，且揭示 correlation-selection gap\\\\n    - ReASC (2026, arxiv 2601.02970) — reliability-aware adaptive self-consistency; 对比：仍限于 reasoning 离散答案\\\\n    - Atomic Self-Consistency (EMNLP 2024) — 子声明级一致性 for long-form text; 对比：我们在 entity-set 级别做结构化度量\\\\n    - **Nédellec & Xia (Genomics Informatics, 2026-02, PMC12888769)** — \\\\\\\"Consistency–accuracy correlation in hard-prompted LLMs for entity and relation extraction\\\\\\\"。最直接竞争者：4 LLMs 在植物健康 NER/RE 上研究 consistency-accuracy 相关性。**关键差异**：(a) 他们用 surface-level string matching，自承\\\\\\\"overlooks semantically consistent outputs\\\\\\\"，我们的 SJ 正好解决此问题; (b) 不研究 best-of-N selection; (c) 单领域 hard-prompted (T=0); (d) 无多信号家族对比。论文 Intro 可引用其 limitation 作为 SJ 的动机\\\\n  - **[2025-2026 新颖性自查补充 — 应引]**:\\\\n    - UQ for LLMs Survey (ACL Findings 2025) — 最全面 LLM 不确定性综述\\\\n    - UQ+Calibration Survey (arxiv 2503.15850, 2025) — 第二篇综合综述\\\\n    - Semantic Entropy Probes (2024) — 低成本不确定性估计\\\\n    - Latent Self-Consistency (2025, arxiv 2508.18395) — embedding 空间一致性\\\\n    - Ranked Voting SC (2025, arxiv 2505.10772) — 排名投票变体，仍限于推理任务\\\\n    - Know Your Limits: Abstention Survey (TACL 2025) — 选择性预测综述，与 Claim 1 selective prediction 角度直接相关\\\\n    - Confidence Estimation Survey (NAACL 2024, arxiv 2311.08298) — LLM 置信度估计全景定位\\\\n    - **CoCoA (Vashurin et al., NeurIPS 2025, arxiv 2502.04964)** — MBR 框架融合 confidence+consistency 做 UQ，在 QA/摘要/翻译上评估。与我们 exp-016 ensemble (SJ+logprob) 对标，但未涉及 structured IE\\\\n    - **Vashurin et al. (arxiv 2510.20460, 2025)** — VCE/MSP/Sample Consistency/CoCoA 四方法系统评估，任务不含 structured IE\\\\n  - **需新增 Related Work 小节**:\\\\n    - \\\\\\\"Test-Time Scaling & Sample Selection\\\\\\\" — 将 CISC/Self-Certainty/ReASC 集中讨论，明确 positioning：现有工作均面向 reasoning/QA 的离散答案，我们首次在 structured IE 上做系统信号对比并发现 correlation-selection gap\\\\n    - \\\\\\\"Consistency for Structured IE\\\\\\\" — 引 Nédellec & Xia 2026 作为同期工作，显式对比：surface matching vs structural metrics (SJ)；他们的 limitation 是我们的 motivation\\\\n- **Priority**: P0, Paper Agent handles\\\\n\\\\n### R11: \\\\\\\"SJ+logprob ensemble missing\\\\\\\" (Dream 2 — both reviewers)\\\\n- **Status**: ✅ RESOLVED by exp-016. Results:\\\\n  - Ensemble AUROC: NER 0.735 (+3.5pp over best single), RE 0.719 (−0.2pp, no improvement)\\\\n  - Linear combo: SJ+voting best at α=0.3 (NER ρ=0.489); RE α=0.0 (pure voting)\\\\n  - FK negative coefficient in logistic regression → surface agreement is net-negative when structural signals present\\\\n  - **Interpretation**: voting_conf captures nearly all discriminative information; ensemble adds marginal value on NER, none on RE. Supports the \\\\\\\"structural consistency family\\\\\\\" narrative.\\\\n- **Priority**: DONE — write into paper with honest interpretation\\\\n\\\\n### R12: \\\\\\\"No supervised upper bound / reward model comparison\\\\\\\" (Dream 2)\\\\n- **Mitigation**: Simple logistic regression on [SJ, FK, logprob, voting_conf, EM] as proxy for trained verifier (included in exp-016). Full reward model training out of scope for this paper — discuss as future work.\\\\n- **Priority**: P1 (partially addressed by exp-016 ensemble)\\\\n\\\\n### R13: \\\\\\\"Headroom capture rate low (37% NER, 11% RE)\\\\\\\" (Dream 2)\\\\n- **Mitigation**: Discuss explicitly in paper. Contextualize vs other unsupervised TTS methods. Argue that the information-theoretic ceiling for unsupervised signals is unknown — our result establishes a baseline.\\\\n- **Priority**: P0, paper writing\\\\n\\\\n### R14: \\\\\\\"Cross-model validation thin — need more than 2×8B\\\\\\\" (Dream 2)\\\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-017 (LLaMA on CoNLL, P1). 70B model infeasible within GPU budget. Acknowledge as limitation.\\\\n- **Priority**: P1\\\\n\\\\n### R15: \\\\\\\"Log-prob definition ambiguity — Eq.1 vs best-of-N ranking\\\\\\\" (Dream 2 cross-tier, [W])\\\\n- **Mitigation**: Paper must clarify: log-prob ranks among N candidates (each sample's generation log-prob), not just score greedy output. Cost comparison table must state \\\\\\\"all best-of-N require N forward passes; log-prob adds zero ranking overhead.\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **Priority**: P0, Paper Agent handles\\\\n\\\\n### R16: \\\\\\\"Zero figures — data-rich paper with only tables\\\\\\\" (Dream 2 cross-tier, [W])\\\\n- **Mitigation**: Paper Agent must include ≥3 figures: (1) selection curves (F1 vs N for each signal + oracle), (2) reliability diagram (calibration), (3) N-scaling or cost-effectiveness scatter. Data exists from exp-010/015.\\\\n- **Priority**: P0, Paper Agent handles\\\\n\\\\n### R17: \\\\\\\"Diagnostic framework overclaimed — descriptive not prescriptive\\\\\\\" (Dream 2 cross-tier, [M][W])\\\\n- **Mitigation**: Soften \\\\\\\"diagnostic framework\\\\\\\" to \\\\\\\"diagnostic analysis\\\\\\\" throughout. Current taxonomy (ceiling/floor/compound error) is descriptive. Either formalize with a priori metrics or reframe as empirical analysis.\\\\n- **Priority**: P0, Paper Agent handles\\\\n\\\\n### R18: \\\\\\\"No discussion of when consistency WOULD help\\\\\\\" (Dream 2 cross-tier, [W])\\\\n- **Mitigation**: Add discussion paragraph: consistency should outperform log-prob when (a) model calibration is poor (in-context learning without fine-tuning), (b) log-prob unavailable (closed-source APIs), (c) ensemble/mixture settings, (d) tasks where model is systematically overconfident.\\\\n- **Priority**: P1, Paper Agent handles\\\\n\\\\n### R19: \\\\\\\"Negative result partially predictable for fine-tuned models\\\\\\\" (Dream 2 cross-tier, [W])\\\\n- **Mitigation**: Paper must argue non-obviousness: (a) self-consistency demonstrably helps for reasoning tasks (Wang et al. 2023), (b) structured prediction has internal structure that surface metrics can't capture, (c) the DEGREE of log-prob's strength and the task-complexity boundary are not predictable a priori.\\\\n- **Priority**: P1, Paper Agent handles\\\\n\\\\n## Venue Fit\\\\n\\\\nEMNLP is well-suited for:\\\\n- Systematic empirical comparison of confidence signals for structured prediction\\\\n- Diagnostic findings with principled explanations\\\\n- The \\\\\\\"comparison + boundary condition\\\\\\\" contribution pattern\\\\n\\\\nRisk assessment (post-Dream 2, all tiers 3/6):\\\\n- **Structural risks (三档一致)**: (1) novelty perceived as incremental, (2) related work thin, (3) single RE dataset. Must cite:\\n  - USC (Chen 2024) — empirical differentiation, not just citation\\n  - Semantic uncertainty (Kuhn 2023), calibration (Kadavath 2022, Guo 2017, Desai & Durrett 2020)\\n  - **Conformal prediction for structured outputs**: Fisch et al. 2021, Angelopoulos et al. 2021, Bates et al. 2021\\n  - **Token-level NER uncertainty**: Xiao & Wang 2019, Shelmanov et al. 2021, He et al. 2020\\n  - **Ensemble/MC-dropout**: Lakshminarayanan et al. 2017, Gal & Ghahramani 2016\\n  - **Verifier/reward models**: Cobbe et al. 2021, Lightman et al. 2023\\n  - **TTS scaling theory**: Snell et al. 2024, Brown et al. 2024\\n  - **Structured calibration**: Kuleshov & Liang 2015, Jagannatha & Yu 2022\\n  - **IE confidence**: Culotta & McCallum 2004\\n  - SelfCheckGPT (Manakul 2023), selective prediction (Geifman & El-Yaniv 2017)\\n  - **[2025-2026 新颖性自查补充 — 必引]**:\\n    - CISC (ACL Findings 2025) — confidence-weighted majority vote for reasoning; 对比：我们用 structural similarity 而非 confidence 加权，且面向 set-structured IE 输出\\n    - Self-Certainty Best-of-N (ICLR 2026, arxiv 2502.18581) — KL-divergence reward-free selection; 对比：我们发现 log-prob 是唯一有效 selection signal (与其方向互补)，且揭示 correlation-selection gap\\n    - ReASC (2026, arxiv 2601.02970) — reliability-aware adaptive self-consistency; 对比：仍限于 reasoning 离散答案\\n    - Atomic Self-Consistency (EMNLP 2024) — 子声明级一致性 for long-form text; 对比：我们在 entity-set 级别做结构化度量\\n    - **Nédellec & Xia (Genomics Informatics, 2026-02, PMC12888769)** — \\\"Consistency–accuracy correlation in hard-prompted LLMs for entity and relation extraction\\\"。最直接竞争者：4 LLMs 在植物健康 NER/RE 上研究 consistency-accuracy 相关性。**关键差异**：(a) 他们用 surface-level string matching，自承\\\"overlooks semantically consistent outputs\\\"，我们的 SJ 正好解决此问题; (b) 不研究 best-of-N selection; (c) 单领域 hard-prompted (T=0); (d) 无多信号家族对比。论文 Intro 可引用其 limitation 作为 SJ 的动机\\n  - **[2025-2026 新颖性自查补充 — 应引]**:\\n    - UQ for LLMs Survey (ACL Findings 2025) — 最全面 LLM 不确定性综述\\n    - UQ+Calibration Survey (arxiv 2503.15850, 2025) — 第二篇综合综述\\n    - Semantic Entropy Probes (2024) — 低成本不确定性估计\\n    - Latent Self-Consistency (2025, arxiv 2508.18395) — embedding 空间一致性\\n    - Ranked Voting SC (2025, arxiv 2505.10772) — 排名投票变体，仍限于推理任务\\n    - Know Your Limits: Abstention Survey (TACL 2025) — 选择性预测综述，与 Claim 1 selective prediction 角度直接相关\\n    - Confidence Estimation Survey (NAACL 2024, arxiv 2311.08298) — LLM 置信度估计全景定位\\n    - **CoCoA (Vashurin et al., NeurIPS 2025, arxiv 2502.04964)** — MBR 框架融合 confidence+consistency 做 UQ，在 QA/摘要/翻译上评估。与我们 exp-016 ensemble (SJ+logprob) 对标，但未涉及 structured IE\\n    - **Vashurin et al. (arxiv 2510.20460, 2025)** — VCE/MSP/Sample Consistency/CoCoA 四方法系统评估，任务不含 structured IE\\n  - **需新增 Related Work 小节**:\\n    - \\\"Test-Time Scaling & Sample Selection\\\" — 将 CISC/Self-Certainty/ReASC 集中讨论，明确 positioning：现有工作均面向 reasoning/QA 的离散答案，我们首次在 structured IE 上做系统信号对比并发现 correlation-selection gap\\n    - \\\"Consistency for Structured IE\\\" — 引 Nédellec & Xia 2026 作为同期工作，显式对比：surface matching vs structural metrics (SJ)；他们的 limitation 是我们的 motivation\\n- **Priority**: P0, Paper Agent handles\\n\\n### R11: \\\"SJ+logprob ensemble missing\\\" (Dream 2 — both reviewers)\\n- **Status**: ✅ RESOLVED by exp-016. Results:\\n  - Ensemble AUROC: NER 0.735 (+3.5pp over best single), RE 0.719 (−0.2pp, no improvement)\\n  - Linear combo: SJ+voting best at α=0.3 (NER ρ=0.489); RE α=0.0 (pure voting)\\n  - FK negative coefficient in logistic regression → surface agreement is net-negative when structural signals present\\n  - **Interpretation**: voting_conf captures nearly all discriminative information; ensemble adds marginal value on NER, none on RE. Supports the \\\"structural consistency family\\\" narrative.\\n- **Priority**: DONE — write into paper with honest interpretation\\n\\n### R12: \\\"No supervised upper bound / reward model comparison\\\" (Dream 2)\\n- **Mitigation**: Simple logistic regression on [SJ, FK, logprob, voting_conf, EM] as proxy for trained verifier (included in exp-016). Full reward model training out of scope for this paper — discuss as future work.\\n- **Priority**: P1 (partially addressed by exp-016 ensemble)\\n\\n### R13: \\\"Headroom capture rate low (37% NER, 11% RE)\\\" (Dream 2)\\n- **Mitigation**: Discuss explicitly in paper. Contextualize vs other unsupervised TTS methods. Argue that the information-theoretic ceiling for unsupervised signals is unknown — our result establishes a baseline.\\n- **Priority**: P0, paper writing\\n\\n### R14: \\\"Cross-model validation thin — need more than 2×8B\\\" (Dream 2)\\n- **Mitigation**: exp-017 (LLaMA on CoNLL, P1). 70B model infeasible within GPU budget. Acknowledge as limitation.\\n- **Priority**: P1\\n\\n### R15: \\\"Log-prob definition ambiguity — Eq.1 vs best-of-N ranking\\\" (Dream 2 cross-tier, [W])\\n- **Mitigation**: Paper must clarify: log-prob ranks among N candidates (each sample's generation log-prob), not just score greedy output. /root/autodl-tmp -name \"config.json\" -path \"*merged*\" 2>/dev/null`\n- OOM：降 n_samples 或分批处理\n- 脚本参数不对：先运行 `python code/run_mvp_pilot.py --help` 查看支持的参数\n",

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01TdNERWm2uYyVyduqVNVvJo`</summary>

```text
1	# Paper Framing — struct_self_consist_ie
2	
3	> Updated 2026-05-14 post-exp-016 pivot (D019). CRITICAL NARRATIVE CHANGE: best-of-N selection fails on NER (all signals ≤ greedy). Pivot from "TTS improvement" to "confidence estimation + correlation-selection gap".
4	
5	## Target Venue
6	- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (via ARR May 2026)
7	- Fallback: EMNLP 2026 Findings
8	- Deadline: ARR May cycle, 2026-05-25
9	
10	## Core Claims (3 条)
11	
12	### Claim 1 (Positive — Quality Estimation)
13	Structural consistency signals (soft Jaccard, entity voting confidence) are the strongest unsupervised quality estimators for entity-level IE, significantly outperforming model confidence (log-probability) and surface agreement (FK, exact-match) at instance-level quality prediction. Structural consistency family achieves AUROC≈0.70 vs logprob 0.59 for NER discrimination. SJ and voting confidence are statistically comparable on NER (ρ≈0.39), SJ is temperature-robust (Δρ=±0.01).
14	
15	**Key insight**: The contribution is about a FAMILY of structural consistency signals for QUALITY ESTIMATION (predicting which instances are reliable), not for per-sample selection. SJ and voting_conf both capture entity-across-sample consistency. SJ generalizes to arbitrary structured outputs; voting is simpler.
16	
17	**Evidence** (SciERC, N=8, T=1.0):
18	- NER full (n=529): voting_conf ρ=0.392/τ=0.291/AUROC=0.704, SJ ρ=0.383/τ=0.266/AUROC=0.689, EM ρ=0.294/AUROC=0.640, FK ρ=0.259/AUROC=0.629, logprob ρ=0.140/AUROC=0.591
19	- NER cond: SJ ρ=0.412 (unique signal >0.40)
20	- Structural consistency family (SJ+voting) AUROC ~0.70 vs logprob 0.59 — both ρ and discriminative power confirm dominance
21	- RE full: voting_conf ρ=0.340/AUROC=0.667 > SJ ρ=0.244/AUROC=0.592 (voting more robust with sparse tuples)
22	- Temperature robustness: SJ Δρ=±0.01 across T=0.7/1.0; FK Δρ=±0.06-0.09 (SJ far more stable)
23	
24	### Claim 2 (Novel Finding — Correlation-Selection Gap)
25	Instance-level quality estimation ≠ per-sample selection ability. Despite strong instance-level correlation (ρ≈0.39-0.43), NO unsupervised confidence signal beats greedy decoding for NER best-of-N selection. On RE, only log-probability (+2.4pp over greedy) enables effective selection. The oracle headroom is large (NER +16pp, RE +18pp) but current signals cannot exploit it.
26	
27	**exp-016 evidence** (SciERC, N=8, T=1.0, n=529):
28	- NER best-of-N: greedy 0.6094 > logprob 0.6087 > ensemble 0.6043 > sj 0.6042 > voting 0.5959 > random 0.5796 (oracle 0.7692)
29	- RE best-of-N: logprob 0.3971 > greedy 0.3735 > sj 0.3680 > voting 0.3379 ≈ random 0.3380 (oracle 0.5610)
30	- Instance-level AUROC: voting_conf 0.700, SJ 0.689, ensemble 0.735 >> logprob 0.591
31	
32	**Key insight**: Consistency signals are INSTANCE-LEVEL aggregates (one score per instance). They predict which inputs are easy/hard, NOT which sample is best. Log-prob is the only PER-SAMPLE signal and uniquely enables selection on uncertain tasks (RE). This distinction between quality estimation and sample selection is the paper's most practically important finding.
33	
34	**Implications**: (a) For selective prediction / abstention → use structural consistency (AUROC 0.70+). (b) For best-of-N selection → use logprob, but only on tasks where greedy is suboptimal (RE). (c) For NER, greedy is near-optimal → TTS offers no advantage with current signals.
35	
36	### Claim 3 (Diagnostic — task-complexity boundary)
37	The effectiveness of consistency-based confidence is governed by task structural complexity: compound error propagation in relational tasks degrades the consistency-accuracy correlation on the full instance set, but the signal remains valid within the model's capability range. This boundary applies across all confidence signals tested.
38	
39	**MVP evidence**: RE full rho_sj=0.234, RE cond rho_sj=0.372 (SJ>FK +11.8pp). Component decomposition shows all RE components equally weak (head_sj rho=0.210). T=0.7 naturally separates knowledge-gap vs uncertainty errors.
40	
41	**exp-005 evidence**: Conditional filtering dwarfs temperature tuning — RE SJ cond lift +0.07~0.14 vs temperature effect ±0.01. T=1.0 preserves more oracle headroom (RE: 0.167 vs 0.131) and diversity (1-SJ: 0.389 vs 0.313). Recommendation: T=1.0 + conditional filtering, not temperature optimization.
42	
43	**exp-001 N-scaling evidence** (SciERC, n=200 subset, ⚠️ needs full-set confirmation):
44	- RE N=16: voting_conf ρ=0.448 (vs N=8 0.340, +0.108), SJ ρ=0.333 (vs 0.244, +0.089), all signals improve +0.07~0.10
45	- NER N=16: flat (already saturated at N=8) — voting_conf 0.401 vs 0.392
46	- **Key insight**: The task-complexity boundary is partially a SAMPLE BUDGET boundary. Compound error propagation increases per-instance variance, requiring more samples (N=16 vs N=8) to average out noise. This makes Claim 2 more nuanced: RE consistency fails at N=8 not because the signal is absent, but because it's noisier and needs more samples to manifest.
47	- ⚠️ Caveat: exp-001 ran on 200/529 instances. Full-set replication needed before claiming in paper.
48	
49	## Story Spine
50	
```

          " **Existing approaches & gaps**: Self-consistency in reasoning uses exact-match voting (Wang et al. 2023). USC (Chen et al. 2024) uses LLM-as-judge. For structured prediction, no systematic comparison exists across confidence signal families. More importantly, prior work conflates quality estimation with selection — assuming that high correlation with accuracy translates to effective best-of-N selection.",
          " ",
          " **Our framing**: We systematically compare five confidence signals across four families for IE: (1) model confidence (log-probability), (2) surface agreement (FK, exact-match), (3) set-aware structural similarity (SJ), (4) entity-level majority voting. We make three findings:",
          "-1. Structural consistency (SJ/voting_conf) provides the strongest quality estimation (AUROC 0.70 vs logprob 0.59)",
          "+1. Structural consistency (voting_conf/SJ) provides the strongest quality estimation (AUROC 0.73 vs logprob 0.56)",
          " 2. However, a **correlation-selection gap** exists: despite strong instance-level correlation, NO signal beats greedy decoding for NER selection; only logprob helps for RE (+2.4pp)",
          " 3. All signals degrade on relational tasks due to compound error propagation",
          " "
        ]
      }

[Director 指令] ## 三实验完成 + 紧急后处理 + GPU 排满

### 1. Registry 更新（立即）
exp_001_seed42_v2、exp_001_seed123_v2、exp_008_re_n16_v2 三个实验已完成，registry 仍显示 RUNNING。立即更新为 DONE，附 Reviewer 核实的关键指标。

### 2. v2 Voting + EM 补算（P0，零 GPU）
v2 的 samples.jsonl 只有 FK/SJ/logprob，**缺 voting_conf 和 exact_match**。必须在 v2 数据上运行 voting + EM 后处理脚本补全。这是 D035 heatmap 统一数据源的前提——如果 v2 N=16 数据要纳入论文，5 信号必须齐全。

对三个 v2 实验都执行：在各自的 samples.jsonl 上计算 voting_conf 和 exact_match，输出更新后的文件。

### 3. Heatmap 重新生成
D035 已批准方案 A（用 exp_015 作为 N=8 统一数据源）。现在 v2 补全 voting+EM 后，N=16 数据也完整了。重新生成 heatmap 时：
- N=8 行用 exp_015 数据
- N=16 行用 v2 数据（补全 voting+EM 后）
- 确保所有值来源清晰标注

### 4. GPU 利用率 — 7/8 空闲不可接受
当前只有 exp-017 在 cuda:0 跑（autodl-5090）。cuda:1-3 已释放，gpu-5090-4x-2 四张卡全空。

**立即列出所有可排的任务**，包括但不限于：
- exp-017 训练完成后的 LLaMA 推理任务（可以先准备代码/数据）
- 任何 experiment_plan.yaml 中 planned 但未跑的 P1 实验
- v2 voting+EM 后处理可以并行在多个 Worker 上跑
- 如果确实无实验可跑，铺 ablation（如不同 N 值的 scaling curve、更多 seed）

5 分钟内汇报 GPU 排期方案。

_source line 326 · 2026-05-14T15:11:51.030Z_

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   This is an ML research project management session for `struct_self_consist_ie`, targeting EMNLP 2026 via ARR May (DDL 2026-05-25, 11 days away). The AI agent manages experiment scheduling, worker dispatch, registry maintenance, and Director communication. The session involved:
   - Session-start initialization (registry sync, cron reconcile, status review)
   - Processing multiple worker completions (zombie cleanup, AUROC bugfix, paper figures, exp-009/010 analyses, heatmap audit)
   - Responding to Director instructions: GPU utilization (8×RTX 5090 across 2 servers), experiment prioritization, heatmap data source unification (D035), paper_framing.md updates
   - The most recent Director instruction requires: (a) updating 3 v2 experiment registries from RUNNING→DONE, (b) computing missing voting_conf and exact_match on v2 samples.jsonl, (c) regenerating heatmap with N=16 row, (d) filling 7/8 idle GPUs with tasks, (e) reporting GPU排期 plan in 5 minutes

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Structural consistency signals for IE: Soft Jaccard (SJ), Fleiss' Kappa (FK), Entity Voting Confidence, Exact Match (EM), Log-probability
   - Quality estimation vs best-of-N selection (correlation-selection gap)
   - Spearman ρ, Kendall τ, AUROC as evaluation metrics
   - LoRA fine-tuning (r=32, alpha=64) on Qwen3-8B and LLaMA-3.1-8B
   - Multiple inference runs with different seeds for robustness
   - Data source consistency: all signals must be computed on same inference run's samples
   - exp_015 unified evaluation as authoritative data source (D035 decision)
   - Conditional filtering (gold-nonempty instances only) vs full set evaluation
   - N-scaling (N=1,2,4,8,16 samples per instance)
   - AUROC binarization bug: median-based threshold degenerates when >50% samples have F1=1.0

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/paper_framing.md`
     - Central document defining paper claims, evidence, and reviewer concerns
     - Updated Claim 1 text: changed from "SJ and voting confidence are statistically comparable on NER (ρ≈0.39)" to "Entity voting achieves AUROC 0.73 / ρ=0.50 on NER, SJ achieves AUROC 0.67 / ρ=0.36"
     - Updated Evidence block with exp_015 unified values (NER full/cond, RE full/cond for all 5 signals)
     - Updated R3b from "NER: statistically equivalent" to acknowledging voting leads SJ, SJ retains conditional/temperature-robust strength
     - Updated Story Spine AUROC: "AUROC 0.70 vs logprob 0.59" → "AUROC 0.73 vs logprob 0.56"
     - Updated Headline template with voting ρ=0.50, AUROC 0.73
   
   - `artifacts/heatmap_data_audit.md`
     - Created by worker, documents root cause of data mixing: 3 independent inference runs
     - Contains exp_015 complete values table for all signals × NER/RE × full/cond
     - Documents voting_conf sensitivity to sample composition (Δ=0.110 largest among signals)
     - RE cond SJ=0.372 traced to pilot_005 (T=0.7), not T=1.0 as claimed
   
   - `figures/paper/fig_signal_heatmap.pdf` (and .py, .png)
     - Regenerated with exp_015 unified data for SciERC columns
     - Key changes: Voting NER 0.392→0.502, SJ NER 0.383→0.357, Voting RE 0.340→0.422
     - CoNLL/WNUT-17/LLaMA data unchanged
   
   - `figures/paper/fig_reliability.pdf`
     - Reliability diagram showing ECE: SJ 0.077, Voting 0.071, Logprob 0.113 (NER); Voting 0.128 vs Logprob 0.380 (RE)
   
   - `artifacts/exp_015_metrics_expansion/intermediate/analyze_exp015_metrics_expansion.py`
     - Read during investigation. Key: uses pilot_004 data path but actually points to exp_012_logprob/samples_with_logprobs.jsonl
     - compute_voting_confidence: entity-level majority voting (count > N/2, mean of vote fractions)
     - compute_auroc: manual AUROC with f > median binarization (buggy, fixed in separate worker)
   
   - `ROADMAP.md` — Research dashboard showing confirmed findings, pending items, GPU budget, timeline
   - `report.md` — Auto-generated handover report

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **AUROC binarization bug**: `compute_auroc` used `f > median` for binary labels. When WNUT-17 conditional set had 67.1% F1=1.0, median=1.0, strict `>` produced empty positive set → AUROC defaulted to 0.5. Fix: fallback to `>= median` vs `< median` when degenerate split detected. Impact: WNUT-17 conditional logprob AUROC 0.5000→0.7092. Modified 4 files: evaluation.py, analyze_logprob_baseline.py, analyze_exact_match_voting.py, analyze_entity_voting.py.
   
   - **Heatmap data source mixing**: Figure used 3 different inference runs' data. Voting Conf ρ showed 0.39 (from pilot_004 run) vs 0.502 (from exp_012 re-inference run). Root cause: LLM sampling randomness produces different outputs even with same config. Fix: D035 approved using exp_015 (single inference run) as sole data source.
   
   - **exp_015 metrics missing from registry**: exp_015 had conclusion=success but empty metrics in registry. Worker completed analysis but metrics weren't stored. Actual output exists on server at output/exp015_metrics_expansion/.
   
   - **experiment_plan.yaml stale registry_ids**: Many experiments completed in registry under different IDs than plan expected. Caused Director to issue redundant instructions for already-completed experiments. Identified and reported to Director.
   
   - **WNUT-17 worker cancelled**: Spawned WNUT-17 redo worker per D028, then Director's D035 said skip ("D029 boundary case study framing 成立"). Cancelled worker_exp_wnut17_redo_downsample_20260514T143407.
   
   - **exp-009/010 workers already running**: Tried to spawn but got "already active" error. Workers had been started by a prior session.
   
   - **Edit tool "file not read" error**: Attempted to edit paper_framing.md after reading it via read_project_file MCP tool, but Edit tool requires Read tool specifically. Fixed by using Read tool first, then Edit.

5. Problem Solving:
   - Resolved heatmap data integrity issue through systematic audit (worker read server files, compared data sources, traced discrepancies to root cause)
   - Identified that voting_conf >> SJ (not ≈SJ) when measured on same inference run, requiring paper narrative adjustment
   - Managed GPU utilization across 2 servers (autodl-5090 and bjb1-21071) with 4 GPUs each
   - Coordinated 10+ workers across GPU and CPU tasks simultaneously
   - Updated paper_framing.md to reflect corrected data while preserving document structure

6. All User Messages:
   - `[WORKER DONE] worker_ops_bjb1_status_zombie_cleanup_20260514T142422` — Initial trigger for session
   - `Continue from where you left off.` (multiple times — system continuation prompts)
   - `[Director 指令] [Director 综合指令]` — Comprehensive GPU allocation plan (8 experiments across 8 GPUs, WNUT-17 P0, exp-001 3 seeds, exp-007 LLaMA check, exp-012, exp-008, exp-013/014 CPU)
   - `[WORKER DONE] worker_paper_figure_fig_signal_heatmap_20260514T142903` — Heatmap v1 complete
